192	
193	<!-- Edit/Add Modal -->
194	<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalOverlay" onclick="closeModal()">
195	  <div class="modal" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
196	    <h3 id="modalTitle">Edit Translation</h3>
197	    <input type="hidden" id="editId">
198	    <label for="editLang">Language Code</label>
199	    <input type="text" id="editLang" placeholder="e.g. en, cs, ja" maxlength="10">
200	    <label for="editKey">Key</label>
201	    <input type="text" id="editKey" placeholder="e.g. legend_safe">
202	    <label for="editValue">Value</label>
203	    <textarea id="editValue" placeholder="Translation text..."></textarea>
204	    <div class="btn-row">
205	      <button class="btn-cancel" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
206	      <button class="btn-save" onclick="saveTranslation()">Save</button>
207	    </div>
208	  </div>
209	</div>
210	
211	<script>
212	const PASSWORD = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('password') || '';
213	const AUTH_PARAM = PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';
214	
215	let currentSort = 'key';
216	let currentOrder = 'asc';
217	let currentSearch = '';
218	let currentLang = '';
219	let currentOffset = 0;
220	let currentLimit = 50;
221	let totalRows = 0;
222	let availableLanguages = [];
223	let currentData = []; // Store fetched rows for edit lookups
224	
225	function buildAdminTabs() {
226	  const tabs = [
227	    { label: 'Users', href: '/admin/users' },
228	    { label: 'Uploads', href: '/admin/uploads' },
229	    { label: 'MCP Analytics', href: '/admin/mcp' },
230	    { label: 'Realtime', href: '/admin/realtime' },
231	    { label: 'Translations', href: '/admin/translations', active: true }
232	  ];
233	  const container = document.getElementById('adminTabs');
234	  tabs.forEach(t => {
235	    const href = t.href + (PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '');
236	    container.innerHTML += `<a href="${href}" class="${t.active ? 'active' : ''}">${t.label}</a>`;
237	  });
238	}
239	
240	function doSearch() {
241	  currentSearch = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.trim();
242	  currentOffset = 0;
243	  fetchData();
244	}
245	
246	function clearSearch() {
247	  document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
248	  currentSearch = '';
249	  currentOffset = 0;
250	  fetchData();
251	}
252	
253	function filterByLang() {
254	  currentLang = document.getElementById('langFilter').value;
255	  currentOffset = 0;
256	  fetchData();
257	}
258	
259	function sortBy(col) {
260	  if (currentSort === col) {
261	    currentOrder = currentOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
262	  } else {
263	    currentSort = col;
264	    currentOrder = col === 'key' || col === 'language_code' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
265	  }
266	  currentOffset = 0;
267	  fetchData();
268	  updateSortIndicators();
269	}
270	
271	function updateSortIndicators() {
272	  document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
273	    th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');
274	  });
275	  // Find the matching th
276	  document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
277	    const col = th.getAttribute('onclick')?.match(/sortBy\('(\w+)'\)/)?.[1];
278	    if (col === currentSort) {
279	      th.classList.add(currentOrder);
280	    }
281	  });
282	}
283	
284	function escapeHtml(str) {
285	  const div = document.createElement('div');
286	  div.textContent = str;
287	  return div.innerHTML;
288	}
289	
290	function formatDate(val) {
291	  if (!val) return '';
292	  try {
293	    const d = new Date(val);
294	    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(val);
295	    return d.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, '');
296	  } catch { return String(val); }
297	}
298	
299	async function fetchData() {
300	  const loading = document.getElementById('loading');
301	  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');
302	  const noData = document.getElementById('noData');
303	
304	  loading.style.display = 'block';
305	  table.style.display = 'none';
306	  noData.style.display = 'none';
307	
308	  let url = `/api/admin/translations?limit=${currentLimit}&offset=${currentOffset}&sort=${currentSort}&order=${currentOrder}`;
309	  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
310	  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
311	  if (currentLang) url += '&lang=' + encodeURIComponent(currentLang);
312	
313	  try {
314	    const resp = await fetch(url);
315	    if (!resp.ok) {
316	      if (resp.status === 401) {
317	        loading.textContent = 'Unauthorized - please login as admin or provide password';
318	        return;
319	      }
320	      throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
321	    }
322	
323	    const json = await resp.json();
324	    totalRows = json.total;
325	    const data = json.data || [];
326	
327	    // Populate language dropdown if we have languages
328	    if (json.languages && json.languages.length > 0 && availableLanguages.length === 0) {
329	      availableLanguages = json.languages;
330	      const select = document.getElementById('langFilter');
331	      json.languages.forEach(lang => {
332	        const opt = document.createElement('option');
333	        opt.value = lang;
334	        opt.textContent = lang;
335	        if (lang === currentLang) opt.selected = true;
336	        select.appendChild(opt);
337	      });
338	    }
339	
340	    loading.style.display = 'none';
341	
342	    if (data.length === 0) {
343	      noData.style.display = 'block';
344	      document.getElementById('summary').textContent = 'No results';
345	      document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';
346	      return;
347	    }
348	
349	    // Build body
350	    currentData = data;
351	    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
352	    let html = '';
353	    data.forEach((row, idx) => {
354	      const valuePreview = row.value.length > 120 ? row.value.substring(0, 120) + '...' : row.value;
355	      html += `<tr>
356	        <td class="col-id">${row.id}</td>
357	        <td class="col-lang"><span class="lang-badge">${escapeHtml(row.language_code)}</span></td>
358	        <td class="col-key">${escapeHtml(row.key)}</td>
359	        <td class="col-value" title="Click to edit" onclick="editByIndex(${idx})">${escapeHtml(valuePreview)}</td>
360	        <td class="col-date">${formatDate(row.updated_at)}</td>
361	        <td class="col-actions">
362	          <button class="btn-edit" onclick="editByIndex(${idx})">Edit</button>
363	          <button class="btn-delete" onclick="deleteByIndex(${idx})">Del</button>
364	        </td>
365	      </tr>`;
366	    });
367	    tbody.innerHTML = html;
368	    table.style.display = 'table';
369	    updateSortIndicators();
370	
371	    const page = Math.floor(currentOffset / currentLimit) + 1;
372	    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / currentLimit);
373	    document.getElementById('summary').textContent = `${totalRows} translations | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;
374	    buildPagination(totalPages, page);
375	
376	  } catch (err) {
377	    loading.textContent = 'Error loading data: ' + err.message;
378	  }
379	}
380	
381	function buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {
382	  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');
383	  let html = '';
384	  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(1)">First</button>`;
385	  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage - 1})">Prev</button>`;
386	  let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 3);
387	  let end = Math.min(totalPages, start + 6);
388	  start = Math.max(1, end - 6);
389	  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
390	    html += `<button class="${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="goToPage(${i})">${i}</button>`;
391	  }
392	  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage + 1})">Next</button>`;
393	  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${totalPages})">Last</button>`;
394	  html += ` <select onchange="changeLimit(this.value)">`;
395	  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {
396	    html += `<option value="${n}" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;
397	  });
398	  html += `</select>`;
399	  container.innerHTML = html;
400	}
401	
402	function goToPage(page) {
403	  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;
404	  fetchData();
405	}
406	
407	function changeLimit(val) {
408	  currentLimit = parseInt(val);
409	  currentOffset = 0;
410	  fetchData();
411	}
412	
413	function editByIndex(idx) {
414	  const row = currentData[idx];
415	  if (!row) return;
416	  showEditModal(row.id, row.language_code, row.key, row.value);
417	}
418	
419	function deleteByIndex(idx) {
420	  const row = currentData[idx];
421	  if (!row) return;
422	  deleteTranslation(row.id, row.language_code, row.key);
423	}
424	
425	function showEditModal(id, lang, key, value) {
426	  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Edit Translation';
427	  document.getElementById('editId').value = id;
428	  document.getElementById('editLang').value = lang;
429	  document.getElementById('editLang').readOnly = true;
430	  document.getElementById('editKey').value = key;
431	  document.getElementById('editKey').readOnly = true;
432	  document.getElementById('editValue').value = value;
433	  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');
434	  document.getElementById('editValue').focus();
435	}
436	
437	function showAddModal() {
438	  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Add Translation';
439	  document.getElementById('editId').value = '';
440	  document.getElementById('editLang').value = currentLang || '';
441	  document.getElementById('editLang').readOnly = false;
442	  document.getElementById('editKey').value = '';
443	  document.getElementById('editKey').readOnly = false;
444	  document.getElementById('editValue').value = '';
445	  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');
446	  document.getElementById('editLang').focus();
447	}
448	
449	function closeModal() {
450	  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('visible');
451	}
452	
453	async function saveTranslation() {
454	  const id = document.getElementById('editId').value;
455	  const lang = document.getElementById('editLang').value.trim();
456	  const key = document.getElementById('editKey').value.trim();
457	  const value = document.getElementById('editValue').value;
458	
459	  if (!lang || !key) {
460	    alert('Language code and key are required');
461	    return;
462	  }
463	
464	  try {
465	    let url, method, body;
466	    if (id) {
467	      // Update existing
468	      url = `/api/admin/translations/${id}`;
469	      if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
470	      method = 'PUT';
471	      body = JSON.stringify({ value });
472	    } else {
473	      // Create new
474	      url = '/api/admin/translations';
475	      if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
476	      method = 'POST';
477	      body = JSON.stringify({ language_code: lang, key, value });
478	    }
479	
480	    const resp = await fetch(url, {
481	      method,
482	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
483	      body
484	    });
485	
486	    if (!resp.ok) {
487	      const text = await resp.text();
488	      throw new Error(text || 'HTTP ' + resp.status);
489	    }
490	
491	    closeModal();
492	    fetchData();
493	  } catch (err) {
494	    alert('Save failed: ' + err.message);
495	  }
496	}
497	
498	async function deleteTranslation(id, lang, key) {
499	  if (!confirm(`Delete translation ${lang}.${key}?`)) return;
500	
501	  let url = `/api/admin/translations/${id}`;
502	  if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
503	
504	  try {
505	    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
506	    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
507	    fetchData();
508	  } catch (err) {
509	    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);
510	  }
511	}
512	
513	async function reloadTranslations() {
514	  let url = '/api/admin/translations/reload';
515	  if (PASSWORD) url += '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
516	
517	  try {
518	    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST' });
519	    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
520	    const json = await resp.json();
521	    alert(json.message || 'Translations reloaded!');
522	  } catch (err) {
523	    alert('Reload failed: ' + err.message);
524	  }
525	}
526	
527	// Keyboard shortcut: Ctrl+S to save in modal
528	document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
529	  if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 's') {
530	    if (document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.contains('visible')) {
531	      e.preventDefault();
532	      saveTranslation();
533	    }
534	  }
535	  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
536	    closeModal();
537	  }
538	});
539	
540	buildAdminTabs();
541	fetchData();
542	</script>
543	</div><!-- .page-content -->
544	<script>
545	(function() {
546	  function applyLabel() {
547	    var btn = document.getElementById('theme-toggle');
548	    if (btn) btn.textContent = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? '\u2600\uFE0F Light Mode' : '\uD83C\uDF19 Dark Mode';
549	  }
550	  applyLabel();
551	  window.toggleTheme = function() {
552	    var next = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
553	    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next);
554	    localStorage.setItem('safecastDocTheme', next);
555	    applyLabel();
556	  };
557	})();
558	</script>
559	</body>
560	</html>

  "content": "// admin_anomaly.go — admin handlers for anomaly detection config and results.\n//\n// Routes (registered in main.go):\n//   GET  /admin/anomaly                     — serve HTML page\n//   GET  /api/admin/anomaly/config          — read current config\n//   POST /api/admin/anomaly/config          — save config\n//   GET  /api/admin/anomaly/data            — paginated anomaly summaries from DuckLake\n//   POST /api/admin/anomaly/rerun/{trackID} — re-run analysis for one track\n\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"strconv\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"sync\"\n)\n\n// anomalySettings holds the runtime anomaly detection configuration.\ntype anomalySettings struct {\n\tSigmaThreshold float64 `json:\"sigma_threshold\"`\n\tMinDoserate    float64 `json:\"min_doserate\"`\n\tMaxDoserate    float64 `json:\"max_doserate\"`\n\tMaxAnomalyIDs  int     `json:\"max_anomaly_ids\"`\n}\n\nvar (\n\tanomalyConfig   anomalySettings\n\tanomalyConfigMu sync.RWMutex\n)\n\nfunc init() {\n\tanomalyConfig = anomalySettings{\n\t\tSigmaThreshold: 3.0,\n\t\tMinDoserate:    0.0,\n\t\tMaxDoserate:    10000.0,\n\t\tMaxAnomalyIDs:  50,\n\t}\n}\n\n// getAnomalyConfig returns a safe copy of the current config.\nfunc getAnomalyConfig() anomalySettings {\n\tanomalyConfigMu.RLock()\n\tdefer anomalyConfigMu.RUnlock()\n\treturn anomalyConfig\n}\n\n// initAnomalyConfig creates the anomaly_config table (if absent) and loads\n// the stored values into the in-memory config. Called once at server startup.\nfunc initAnomalyConfig() {\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// Create table with defaults if it doesn't exist.\n\t_, err := db.DB.Exec(`\n\t\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anomaly_config (\n\t\t\tid               INTEGER PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT 1,\n\t\t\tsigma_threshold  DOUBLE PRECISION    DEFAULT 3.0,\n\t\t\tmin_doserate     DOUBLE PRECISION    DEFAULT 0.0,\n\t\t\tmax_doserate     DOUBLE PRECISION    DEFAULT 10000.0,\n\t\t\tmax_anomaly_ids  INTEGER             DEFAULT 50\n\t\t)`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly-config] create table: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// Ensure exactly one row exists.\n\t_, err = db.DB.Exec(`\n\t\tINSERT INTO anomaly_config (id, sigma_threshold, min_doserate, max_doserate, max_anomaly_ids)\n\t\tVALUES (1, 3.0, 0.0, 10000.0, 50)\n\t\tON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly-config] seed row: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tloadAnomalyConfig()\n}\n\n// loadAnomalyConfig reads from PostgreSQL and updates the in-memory config.\nfunc loadAnomalyConfig() {\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tvar cfg anomalySettings\n\terr := db.DB.QueryRow(`\n\t\tSELECT sigma_threshold, min_doserate, max_doserate, max_anomaly_ids\n\t\tFROM anomaly_config WHERE id = 1`).\n\t\tScan(&cfg.SigmaThreshold, &cfg.MinDoserate, &cfg.MaxDoserate, &cfg.MaxAnomalyIDs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly-config] load: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tanomalyConfigMu.Lock()\n\tanomalyConfig = cfg\n\tanomalyConfigMu.Unlock()\n\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly-config] loaded: sigma=%.1f min=%.2f max=%.0f maxIDs=%d\",\n\t\tcfg.SigmaThreshold, cfg.MinDoserate, cfg.MaxDoserate, cfg.MaxAnomalyIDs)\n}\n\n// adminAnomalyConfigGetHandler — GET /api/admin/anomaly/config\nfunc adminAnomalyConfigGetHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(getAnomalyConfig()) //nolint:errcheck\n}\n\n// adminAnomalyConfigSaveHandler — POST /api/admin/anomaly/config\nfunc adminAnomalyConfigSaveHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Database not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tvar payload anomalySettings\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&payload); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Invalid JSON\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// Sanity-check inputs.\n\tif payload.SigmaThreshold < 1.0 || payload.SigmaThreshold > 10.0 {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"sigma_threshold must be between 1.0 and 10.0\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif payload.MinDoserate < 0 || payload.MinDoserate >= payload.MaxDoserate {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"min_doserate must be >= 0 and less than max_doserate\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif payload.MaxAnomalyIDs < 1 || payload.MaxAnomalyIDs > 500 {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"max_anomaly_ids must be between 1 and 500\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t_, err := db.DB.Exec(`\n\t\tUPDATE anomaly_config\n\t\tSET sigma_threshold=$1, min_doserate=$2, max_doserate=$3, max_anomaly_ids=$4\n\t\tWHERE id=1`,\n\t\tpayload.SigmaThreshold, payload.MinDoserate, payload.MaxDoserate, payload.MaxAnomalyIDs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly-config] save: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Save failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// Reload into memory immediately.\n\tloadAnomalyConfig()\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{\"status\": \"ok\"}) //nolint:errcheck\n}\n\n// adminAnomalyDataHandler — GET /api/admin/anomaly/data\n// Returns paginated rows from track_anomaly_summaries in DuckLake.\nfunc adminAnomalyDataHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Analytics not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlimit, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"limit\"))\n\tif limit <= 0 || limit > 500 {\n\t\tlimit = 50\n\t}\n\toffset, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"offset\"))\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\n\tvalidCols := []string{\"track_id\", \"computed_at\", \"reading_count\", \"anomaly_count\", \"mean_doserate\", \"threshold\"}\n\tsortCol := r.URL.Query().Get(\"sort\")\n\tif !isValidColumn(sortCol, validCols) {\n\t\tsortCol = \"computed_at\"\n\t}\n\torder := strings.ToUpper(r.URL.Query().Get(\"order\"))\n\tif order != \"ASC\" {\n\t\torder = \"DESC\"\n\t}\n\n\tsearch := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\"))\n\n\twhereSQL := \"\"\n\tvar args []interface{}\n\tif search != \"\" {\n\t\twhereSQL = fmt.Sprintf(\"WHERE track_id LIKE ?\")\n\t\targs = append(args, \"%\"+search+\"%\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Total count\n\tcountQuery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM track_anomaly_summaries %s\", whereSQL)\n\tvar total int\n\tif err := duckDB.QueryRow(countQuery, args...).Scan(&total); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly-admin] count: %v\", err)\n\t\ttotal = 0\n\t}\n\n\t// Data\n\tdataQuery := fmt.Sprintf(`\n\t\tSELECT track_id, computed_at, reading_count,\n\t\t       mean_doserate, median_doserate, stddev_doserate,\n\t\t       threshold, anomaly_count, anomaly_max, anomaly_min\n\t\tFROM track_anomaly_summaries %s\n\t\tORDER BY %s %s\n\t\tLIMIT %d OFFSET %d`,\n\t\twhereSQL, sortCol, order, limit, offset)\n\n\tqueryArgs := append(args, limit, offset)\n\t// DuckLake doesn't use $n placeholders for LIMIT/OFFSET in this query form,\n\t// so just build those inline — they're safe integers from strconv.Atoi.\n\tdataQuery = fmt.Sprintf(`\n\t\tSELECT track_id, computed_at, reading_count,\n\t\t       mean_doserate, median_doserate, stddev_doserate,\n\t\t       threshold, anomaly_count, anomaly_max, anomaly_min\n\t\tFROM track_anomaly_summaries %s\n\t\tORDER BY %s %s\n\t\tLIMIT %d OFFSET %d`,\n\t\twhereSQL, sortCol, order, limit, offset)\n\tqueryArgs = args\n\n\trows, err := duckDB.Query(dataQuery, queryArgs...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly-admin] query: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Query failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar results []map[string]interface{}\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar (\n\t\t\ttrackID                                      string\n\t\t\tcomputedAt                                   interface{}\n\t\t\treadingCount, anomalyCount                   int\n\t\t\tmeanDoserate, medianDoserate, stddevDoserate float64\n\t\t\tthreshold, anomalyMax, anomalyMin            float64\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(\n\t\t\t&trackID, &computedAt, &readingCount,\n\t\t\t&meanDoserate, &medianDoserate, &stddevDoserate,\n\t\t\t&threshold, &anomalyCount, &anomalyMax, &anomalyMin,\n\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults = append(results, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"track_id\":        trackID,\n\t\t\t\"computed_at\":     computedAt,\n\t\t\t\"reading_count\":   readingCount,\n\t\t\t\"mean_doserate\":   meanDoserate,\n\t\t\t\"median_doserate\": medianDoserate,\n\t\t\t\"stddev_doserate\": stddevDoserate,\n\t\t\t\"threshold\":       threshold,\n\t\t\t\"anomaly_count\":   anomalyCount,\n\t\t\t\"anomaly_max\":     anomalyMax,\n\t\t\t\"anomaly_min\":     anomalyMin,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\tif results == nil {\n\t\tresults = []map[string]interface{}{}\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{ //nolint:errcheck\n\t\t\"data\":  results,\n\t\t\"total\": total,\n\t})\n}\n\n// adminAnomalyRerunHandler — POST /api/admin/anomaly/rerun/{trackID}\n// Triggers re-computation of anomaly analysis for a single track.\nfunc adminAnomalyRerunHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tparts := strings.Split(strings.Trim(r.URL.Path, \"/\"), \"/\")\n\ttrackID := parts[len(parts)-1]\n\tif trackID == \"\" || trackID == \"rerun\" {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"track_id required\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// Fire and don't wait — same pattern as post-upload trigger.\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tsummary, err := computeAnomalies(trackID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly] rerun %s: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := storeAnomalySummary(summary); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly] rerun store %s: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly] rerun %s: %d readings, %d anomalies\", trackID, summary.ReadingCount, summary.AnomalyCount)\n\t}()\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{\"status\": \"queued\", \"track_id\": trackID}) //nolint:errcheck\n}\n"
}

Default: 50</div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n  <div class=\"config-actions\">\n    <button class=\"btn-save-config\" onclick=\"saveConfig()\">Save Settings</button>\n    <span class=\"config-status\" id=\"configStatus\"></span>\n  </div>\n</div>\n\n<!-- Anomaly results table -->\n<div class=\"controls\">\n  <input type=\"text\" id=\"searchInput\" placeholder=\"Search by track ID...\" onkeydown=\"if(event.key==='Enter')doSearch()\">\n  <button onclick=\"doSearch()\">Search</button>\n  <button onclick=\"clearSearch()\">Clear</button>\n  <span class=\"summary\" id=\"summary\"></span>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"table-wrap\">\n  <div class=\"loading\" id=\"loading\">Loading...</div>\n  <table id=\"dataTable\" style=\"display:none\">\n    <thead>\n      <tr>\n        <th class=\"sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('track_id')\">Track ID</th>\n        <th class=\"sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('computed_at')\">Computed At</th>\n        <th class=\"sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('reading_count')\">Readings</th>\n        <th class=\"sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('mean_doserate')\">Mean (µSv/h)</th>\n        <th>Median (µSv/h)</th>\n        <th>Stddev</th>\n        <th>Threshold</th>\n        <th class=\"sortable\" onclick=\"sortBy('anomaly_count')\">Anomalies</th>\n        <th>Max Anomaly</th>\n        <th>Actions</th>\n      </tr>\n    </thead>\n    <tbody id=\"tableBody\"></tbody>\n  </table>\n  <div class=\"no-data\" id=\"noData\" style=\"display:none\">No anomaly summaries found. Upload a track to trigger analysis.</div>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"pagination\" id=\"pagination\"></div>\n\n<script>\nconst PASSWORD = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('password') || '';\nconst AUTH_PARAM = PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';\n\nlet currentSort = 'computed_at';\nlet currentOrder = 'desc';\nlet currentSearch = '';\nlet currentOffset = 0;\nlet currentLimit = 50;\nlet totalRows = 0;\nlet currentData = [];\n\nfunction buildAdminTabs() {\n  const tabs = [\n    { label: 'Users',        href: '/admin/users' },\n    { label: 'Uploads',      href: '/admin/uploads' },\n    { label: 'MCP Analytics',href: '/admin/mcp' },\n    { label: 'Realtime',     href: '/admin/realtime' },\n    { label: 'Translations', href: '/admin/translations' },\n    { label: 'Anomalies',    href: '/admin/anomaly', active: true }\n  ];\n  const container = document.getElementById('adminTabs');\n  tabs.forEach(t => {\n    const href = t.href + (PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '');\n    container.innerHTML += `<a href=\"${href}\" class=\"${t.active ? 'active' : ''}\">${t.label}</a>`;\n  });\n}\n\nasync function loadConfig() {\n  let url = '/api/admin/anomaly/config' + AUTH_PARAM;\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url);\n    if (!resp.ok) return;\n    const cfg = await resp.json();\n    document.getElementById('cfgSigma').value   = cfg.sigma_threshold;\n    document.getElementById('cfgMinDose').value  = cfg.min_doserate;\n    document.getElementById('cfgMaxDose').value  = cfg.max_doserate;\n    document.getElementById('cfgMaxIDs').value   = cfg.max_anomaly_ids;\n  } catch(e) { console.error('loadConfig:', e); }\n}\n\nasync function saveConfig() {\n  const payload = {\n    sigma_threshold: parseFloat(document.getElementById('cfgSigma').value),\n    min_doserate:    parseFloat(document.getElementById('cfgMinDose').value),\n    max_doserate:    parseFloat(document.getElementById('cfgMaxDose').value),\n    max_anomaly_ids: parseInt(document.getElementById('cfgMaxIDs').value, 10)\n  };\n  const status = document.getElementById('configStatus');\n  status.className = 'config-status';\n  status.style.display = 'none';\n  try {\n    let url = '/api/admin/anomaly/config' + AUTH_PARAM;\n    const resp = await fetch(url, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify(payload)\n    });\n    if (!resp.ok) {\n      const text = await resp.text();\n      throw new Error(text || 'HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    }\n    status.textContent = '✓ Settings saved';\n    status.className = 'config-status ok';\n  } catch(e) {\n    status.textContent = '✗ ' + e.message;\n    status.className = 'config-status err';\n  }\n  status.style.display = 'inline-block';\n  setTimeout(() => { status.style.display = 'none'; }, 4000);\n}\n\nfunction doSearch() {\n  currentSearch = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.trim();\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction clearSearch() {\n  document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';\n  currentSearch = '';\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction sortBy(col) {\n  if (currentSort === col) {\n    currentOrder = currentOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';\n  } else {\n    currentSort = col;\n    currentOrder = 'desc';\n  }\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n  updateSortIndicators();\n}\n\nfunction updateSortIndicators() {\n  document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {\n    th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');\n    const col = th.getAttribute('onclick')?.match(/sortBy\\('(\\w+)'\\)/)?.[1];\n    if (col === currentSort) th.classList.add(currentOrder);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction fmt(val, decimals=4) {\n  if (val == null || val === 0) return '—';\n  return Number(val).toFixed(decimals);\n}\n\nfunction formatDate(val) {\n  if (!val) return '';\n  try {\n    const d = new Date(val);\n    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(val);\n    return d.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').replace(/\\.\\d{3}Z$/, '');\n  } catch { return String(val); }\n}\n\nfunction anomalyBadge(count) {\n  if (count === 0) return `<span class=\"badge-ok\">0</span>`;\n  if (count < 5)   return `<span class=\"badge-warn\">${count}</span>`;\n  return `<span class=\"badge-high\">${count}</span>`;\n}\n\nasync function fetchData() {\n  const loading = document.getElementById('loading');\n  const table   = document.getElementById('dataTable');\n  const noData  = document.getElementById('noData');\n  loading.style.display = 'block';\n  table.style.display   = 'none';\n  noData.style.display  = 'none';\n\n  let url = `/api/admin/anomaly/data?limit=${currentLimit}&offset=${currentOffset}&sort=${currentSort}&order=${currentOrder}`;\n  if (PASSWORD)       url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n  if (currentSearch)  url += '&search='   + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url);\n    if (!resp.ok) {\n      if (resp.status === 401) { loading.textContent = 'Unauthorized'; return; }\n      throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    }\n    const json = await resp.json();\n    totalRows   = json.total;\n    currentData = json.data || [];\n    loading.style.display = 'none';\n\n    if (currentData.length === 0) {\n      noData.style.display = 'block';\n      document.getElementById('summary').textContent = 'No results';\n      document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');\n    tbody.innerHTML = currentData.map((row, idx) => `\n      <tr>\n        <td><code>${row.track_id}</code></td>\n        <td>${formatDate(row.computed_at)}</td>\n        <td>${row.reading_count.toLocaleString()}</td>\n        <td>${fmt(row.mean_doserate)}</td>\n        <td>${fmt(row.median_doserate)}</td>\n        <td>${fmt(row.stddev_doserate)}</td>\n        <td>${fmt(row.threshold)}</td>\n        <td>${anomalyBadge(row.anomaly_count)}</td>\n        <td>${row.anomaly_max ? fmt(row.anomaly_max) : '—'}</td>\n        <td>\n          <a class=\"btn-view\" href=\"/trackid/${row.track_id}\" target=\"_blank\">View</a>\n          <button class=\"btn-rerun\" id=\"rerun-${idx}\" onclick=\"rerun('${row.track_id}', ${idx})\">Re-run</button>\n        </td>\n      </tr>`).join('');\n\n    table.style.display = 'table';\n    updateSortIndicators();\n\n    const page = Math.floor(currentOffset / currentLimit) + 1;\n    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / currentLimit);\n    document.getElementById('summary').textContent =\n      `${totalRows} track${totalRows !== 1 ? 's' : ''} analysed | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;\n    buildPagination(totalPages, page);\n\n  } catch(err) {\n    loading.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function rerun(trackID, idx) {\n  const btn = document.getElementById('rerun-' + idx);\n  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = '...'; }\n  let url = `/api/admin/anomaly/rerun/${trackID}` + AUTH_PARAM;\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST' });\n    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    setTimeout(() => fetchData(), 3500); // refresh after analysis completes\n  } catch(e) {\n    alert('Re-run failed: ' + e.message);\n    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Re-run'; }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {\n  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');\n  let html = '';\n  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(1)\">First</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${currentPage - 1})\">Prev</button>`;\n  let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 3);\n  let end   = Math.min(totalPages, start + 6);\n  start = Math.max(1, end - 6);\n  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {\n    html += `<button class=\"${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}\" onclick=\"goToPage(${i})\">${i}</button>`;\n  }\n  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${currentPage + 1})\">Next</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${totalPages})\">Last</button>`;\n  html += ` <select onchange=\"changeLimit(this.value)\">`;\n  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {\n    html += `<option value=\"${n}\" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;\n  });\n  html += `</select>`;\n  container.innerHTML = html;\n}\n\nfunction goToPage(page) {\n  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction changeLimit(val) {\n  currentLimit  = parseInt(val);\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nbuildAdminTabs();\nloadConfig();\nfetchData();\n</script>\n</div><!-- .page-content -->\n<script>\n(function() {\n  function applyLabel() {\n    var btn = document.getElementById('theme-toggle');\n    if (btn) btn.textContent = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? '\\u2600\\uFE0F Light Mode' : '\\uD83C\\uDF19 Dark Mode';\n  }\n  applyLabel();\n  window.toggleTheme = function() {\n    var next = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next);\n    localStorage.setItem('safecastDocTheme', next);\n    applyLabel();\n  };\n})();\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

Clean build. Here's what's ready to test:

---

**Start the server and go to:**
```
http://localhost:8765/admin/anomaly?password=test123
```

**What you'll see:**

- **Config section at the top** — 4 fields (Sigma σ, Min doserate, Max doserate, Max anomaly IDs) with a Save button. Changes take effect immediately on next upload/re-run.

- **Table below** — shows all analysed tracks with: reading count, mean/median/stddev, threshold, anomaly count (colour-coded: green=0, orange=1-4, red=5+), max anomalous value, and per-row **View** (opens track) and **Re-run** (re-analyses on demand) buttons.

**To test end-to-end:**
1. Upload any track file
2. Watch server logs for `[anomaly] <trackID>: N readings, M anomalies`
3. Refresh the Anomalies admin page — the track row should appear
4. Try changing sigma to `2.0` and hit Re-run — more anomalies should be flagged

Two things I can see from the screenshots:

1. **The Translations page has no Anomalies tab** — the old binary is still running, the new one hasn't been started yet.

```bash
# Stop current server (Ctrl+C in the terminal running it), then:
/usr/local/go/bin/go build -o safecast-new-map ./cmd/unified-server/ && bash local-server-config.sh
```

Then go directly to:
```
http://localhost:8765/admin/anomaly?password=test123

> AGENT

Found it — the running process is using the **old deleted binary**. The `go build` replaced the file but the old process is still running with the original code. The server needs to be restarted:

```bash
kill 6998
```

Then in the terminal where the server runs, start it again:

```bash
bash local-server-config.sh
```

Then go to:
```
http://localhost:8765/admin/anomaly?password=admin123
```

(Note: password is `admin123`, not `test123` — that's what's in your `local-server-config.sh`)

1	# Safecast New Map - Project Memory
2	
3	## Git Workflow
4	- [Always use branch → PR → merge, never push directly to main](feedback_git_workflow.md)
5	
6	## Monorepo Structure (Mar 2026)
7	- MCP server and web-chat merged into this repo under `cmd/mcp-server/` and `cmd/web-chat/`
8	- All 3 binaries built and deployed by single `deploy.yml` workflow
9	- safecast-map-MCP repo archived on GitHub
10	- Build all: `go build -o safecast-new-map . && go build -o safecast-mcp ./cmd/mcp-server/ && go build -o safecast-web-chat ./cmd/web-chat/`
11	
12	## CloudFront + Nginx Routing (Mar 2026, updated Apr 2026)
13	- ✅ MCP server: `https://simplemap.safecast.org/mcp-http`
14	- ✅ Combined API docs: `https://simplemap.safecast.org/docs/` (Map API + MCP API tabbed, served by port 8765)
15	- ✅ Map API docs: `https://simplemap.safecast.org/map-api/` (port 8765)
16	- ✅ MCP API docs: `https://simplemap.safecast.org/mcp-api/` (port 8765 — needs explicit nginx location block)
17	- ✅ Web-chat: `https://simplemap.safecast.org/assistant/`
18	- SSL cert includes both `simplemap.safecast.org` and `origin-simplemap.safecast.org`
19	- Nginx configs: `/etc/nginx/sites-available/origin-simplemap.safecast.org` AND `simplemap.safecast.org` — both must be updated together
20	- **CRITICAL:** Use specific `location = /api/endpoint` rules for MCP, NOT a broad `location /api/` — a broad rule breaks map-server routes like `/api/auth/` and `/api/spectrum/`
21	- **CRITICAL:** `/mcp-api/` is served by port 8765 (unified server), NOT port 3333
22	- See `docs/cloudfront-mcp-setup.md` for full routing table
23	
24	## User Upload Tracking (Feb 2026)
25	- ✅ Uploads are linked to logged-in users via `internal_user_id` field in uploads table
26	- Profile page shows user's upload history at `/profile`
27	- Migration: `migrations/link_historical_uploads_to_users.sql` links historical Safecast imports to users
28	- Local dev server config: `local-server-config.sh` (has all credentials and settings)
29	
30	## Server Startup Command
31	```bash
32	./safecast-new-map \
33	  -db-type pgx \
34	  -db-conn "postgres://postgres:PASSWORD@127.0.0.1:5432/safecast?sslmode=disable" \
35	  -safecast-realtime \
36	  -admin-password "test123" \
37	  -safecast-fetcher \
38	  -smtp-host "smtp.gmail.com" \
39	  -smtp-port 587 \
40	  -smtp-username "oudendijk.biz@gmail.com" \
41	  -smtp-password "APP_PASSWORD" \
42	  -smtp-from "oudendijk.biz@gmail.com" \
43	  -session-secret "YOUR_SESSION_SECRET" \
44	  -base-url "http://localhost:8765" \
45	  -allow-registration
46	```
47	
48	## Key URLs
49	- Admin Users: `http://localhost:8765/admin/users?password=test123`
50	- Admin Uploads: `http://localhost:8765/api/admin/uploads?password=test123`
51	- Admin Translations: `http://localhost:8765/admin/translations?password=test123`
52	
53	## Translations (i18n) — Mar 2026
54	- **Storage:** PostgreSQL `translations` table, loaded into `var translations map[string]map[string]string` at startup
55	- **Seeding:** `seedTranslationsDB()` runs on every startup, inserts missing keys from embedded `translations.json` using `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (never overwrites admin edits)
56	- **Language selection:** `?lang=` URL param → `Accept-Language` header → English fallback (server-side in `getPreferredLanguage()`)
57	- **Performance:** `translationsForLang()` filters `TranslationsJSON` to current lang + English (~30KB vs ~850KB for all 30 langs) — keeps AI widget within Claude's 200K token limit
58	- **Branding:** "Safecast" must stay untranslated in all languages — never translate as ChiCha/チチャ etc.
59	- **Admin UI:** `/admin/translations` — edit/add/delete, filter by language, "Reload into Memory" button
60	- **Editing translations in production DB:** `psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d safecast -c "UPDATE translations SET value='...' WHERE language_code='xx' AND key='yy'"` then restart service
61	
62	## Database
63	- Type: PostgreSQL (pgx driver)
64	- Config: `/etc/postgresql/16/main/postgresql.conf`
65	- Running migration: `psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d safecast -f migrations/filename.sql`
66	- **Security:** `listen_addresses = 'localhost'` only — never expose port 5432 publicly
67	- Firewall blocks port 5432 externally via iptables-persistent (`/etc/iptables/rules.v4`)
68	
69	## Analytics / DuckLake (Mar 2026)
70	- **Architecture:** In-memory DuckDB + DuckLake (PostgreSQL catalog + Parquet data files)
71	- Both unified-server and MCP server attach the same DuckLake catalog concurrently
72	- **DuckDB driver:** `github.com/duckdb/duckdb-go/v2` (bundles DuckDB v1.5.0)
73	- **DuckLake catalog DB:** `ducklake_catalog` in PostgreSQL, user `ducklake_rw`
74	- **Parquet data path:** `/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/`
75	- **Env vars:** `DUCKLAKE_PG_URL`, `DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH`
76	- **Tables (shared via DuckLake):** `chat_questions`, `mcp_query_log`, `mcp_ai_query_log`
77	- **DuckLake limitations:** No sequences, no RETURNING, no multi-statement DDL
78	- **Port layout:** unified-server=8765 (HTTP) + 3333 (MCP), web-chat=3334, chat-proxy=3335, MCP server=3336
79	- Old `analytics.duckdb` files removed; data migrated to DuckLake
80	
81	## Authentication
82	- Supports both URL password (`?password=xxx`) and session-based admin auth
83	- Users with `is_admin=true` can access admin pages when logged in
84	- API key auth via `X-API-Key` header or `?api_key=` query parameter
85	
86	## Build
87	- Rebuild required after HTML changes (files are embedded)
88	- **Correct command:** `/usr/local/go/bin/go build -o safecast-new-map ./cmd/unified-server/`
89	- `safecast-new-map` is built from `cmd/unified-server/`, NOT root (root `main.go` has broken pkg/api pkg/web imports)
90	- HTML edits go in `cmd/unified-server/public_html/map.html` — do NOT edit root `public_html/map.html` (has broken Go templates)
91	- Local server run: `bash local-server-config.sh`
92	
93	## Production Deployment (simplemap.safecast.org)
94	**Server IP:** 65.108.24.131 (simplemap.safecast.org domain points to CloudFront)
95	**SSH Key:** ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy
96	
97	**IMPORTANT: Always follow this exact order:**
98	1. Stop the service: `ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl stop safecast-new-map"`
99	2. Sync the binary: `rsync -avP -e "ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy" ./safecast-new-map root@65.108.24.131:/usr/local/bin/`
100	3. Start the service: `ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl start safecast-new-map"`
101	4. Verify: `ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl status safecast-new-map"`
102	
103	**GitHub Actions Deployment:**
104	- Automatic deployment on push to main branch — builds and deploys all 3 binaries
105	- Secrets required: DEPLOY_SSH_KEY, AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID, AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, DATABASE_URL, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
106	
107	## Codeberg Mirror (Mar 2026)
108	- Repo mirrored at https://codeberg.org/Safecast/safecast-new-map
109	- Both GitHub and Codeberg configured as push targets on `origin` — one `git push` hits both
110	- All workflow jobs guarded with `if: github.server_url == 'https://github.com'` — Codeberg Actions not yet active
111	- Migration plan saved in memory: [project_codeberg_migration.md](project_codeberg_migration.md)
112	
113	## RAG / Semantic Cache (Apr 2026)
114	- Feature branch: `feat/rag-semantic-cache`
115	- Embedding strategy: pure Go feature hashing (no external API) — see [project_rag_embedding_decision.md](project_rag_embedding_decision.md)
116	- Upgrade path: Voyage AI (`voyage-3-lite`) if pure Go similarity proves insufficient
117	- New DuckLake tables: `qa_embeddings`, `location_knowledge`
118	- Feedback endpoint: `POST /api/feedback {"chat_id": <int>, "score": 1|-1}`
119

> AGENT

Glad it's working!

---

**Spectral files** — do you mean anomaly detection should also run for spectrum/spectrometry uploads? Currently `triggerAnomalyAnalysis` is skipped for spectrum uploads (`!isSpectrumUpload` check). 

For spectra the data model is different — it's energy channel counts rather than a doserate timeseries, so the 3σ approach would need to work on a different metric. A few options:

1. **Skip spectra entirely** — anomaly detection only applies to dose-rate tracks (current behaviour)
2. **Flag spectral tracks with unusually high total counts or peak channel energy** — simple, same approach
3. **Flag individual spectra within a track that deviate from the track's median spectrum** — more sophisticated

Which direction were you thinking?

> DEVELOPER

3

> AGENT

Good choice — this is the most scientifically useful one. Here's the approach before I code:

**Algorithm:**
1. Load all spectra for the track (via `spectra JOIN markers ON marker_id WHERE trackid=$1`)
2. Normalize each spectrum to counts/second (divide by `live_time_sec`)
3. Compute the **median spectrum** (median count rate per channel across all spectra)
4. For each spectrum compute **cosine similarity** vs the median — this catches shape changes (new isotopes) independent of overall intensity
5. Flag spectra where cosine similarity < configurable threshold (default 0.85)

**What gets added:**
- New DuckLake table `spectrum_anomaly_summaries`
- 2 new config fields in `anomaly_config`: `spectrum_cosine_threshold` (default 0.85) and `spectrum_min_live_time` (default 1.0s — ignore too-short captures)
- New file `spectrum_anomaly.go` with the analysis logic
- Trigger in upload handler for spectrum uploads
- Second section on the `/admin/anomaly` page: config + results table

**Why cosine similarity?** It compares spectral *shape* not absolute counts — so a spectrum from the same source at double the distance (same shape, half counts) scores ~1.0, while a spectrum with a new Cs-137 peak scores much lower. That's exactly what "this spectrum is different from the rest of the track" means physically.

Want me to implement it?

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/spectrum_anomaly.go",
  "content": "// spectrum_anomaly.go — post-upload anomaly detection for spectrum tracks.\n//\n// After a spectrum track upload completes, triggerSpectrumAnomalyAnalysis\n// launches a background goroutine that:\n//  1. Loads all spectra for the track from PostgreSQL (joined via markers).\n//  2. Normalises each spectrum to counts/second (divides by live_time_sec).\n//  3. Computes the median normalised spectrum (median count rate per channel).\n//  4. Computes cosine similarity of each spectrum against the median.\n//  5. Flags spectra whose similarity falls below the configured threshold.\n//  6. Stores the summary in the DuckLake spectrum_anomaly_summaries table.\n//\n// The summary is exposed via GET /api/track/{id}/insights (track_insights.go)\n// and the /admin/anomaly admin page.\n\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"math\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// SpectrumAnomalySummary holds the spectral anomaly analysis for one track.\ntype SpectrumAnomalySummary struct {\n\tTrackID            string                `json:\"track_id\"`\n\tComputedAt         time.Time             `json:\"computed_at\"`\n\tSpectrumCount      int                   `json:\"spectrum_count\"`\n\tAnomalyCount       int                   `json:\"anomaly_count\"`\n\tAnomalySpectrumIDs []int64               `json:\"anomaly_spectrum_ids,omitempty\"`\n\tMedianTotalCounts  float64               `json:\"median_total_counts\"`\n\tCosineThreshold    float64               `json:\"cosine_threshold\"`\n\tAnomalyDetails     []spectrumAnomalyItem `json:\"anomaly_details,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// spectrumAnomalyItem describes one anomalous spectrum.\ntype spectrumAnomalyItem struct {\n\tSpectrumID      int64   `json:\"spectrum_id\"`\n\tMarkerID        int64   `json:\"marker_id\"`\n\tCosineSimilarity float64 `json:\"cosine_similarity\"`\n\tTotalCountsPerSec float64 `json:\"total_counts_per_sec\"`\n}\n\n// triggerSpectrumAnomalyAnalysis launches background spectrum analysis.\n// Safe to call from the upload goroutine — returns immediately.\nfunc triggerSpectrumAnomalyAnalysis(trackID string) {\n\tif trackID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\ttime.Sleep(2 * time.Second)\n\t\tsummary, err := computeSpectrumAnomalies(trackID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[spectrum-anomaly] %s: compute failed: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := storeSpectrumAnomalySummary(summary); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[spectrum-anomaly] %s: store failed: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[spectrum-anomaly] %s: %d spectra, %d anomalies (cosine threshold %.2f)\",\n\t\t\ttrackID, summary.SpectrumCount, summary.AnomalyCount, summary.CosineThreshold)\n\t}()\n}\n\n// spectrumRow holds raw data loaded from PostgreSQL for one spectrum.\ntype spectrumRow struct {\n\tid          int64\n\tmarkerID    int64\n\tchannelsJSON string\n\tliveTimeSec float64\n}\n\n// computeSpectrumAnomalies performs the full analysis for a track.\nfunc computeSpectrumAnomalies(trackID string) (*SpectrumAnomalySummary, error) {\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"database not available\")\n\t}\n\tcfg := getAnomalyConfig()\n\n\trows, err := db.DB.Query(`\n\t\tSELECT s.id, s.marker_id, s.channels, COALESCE(s.live_time_sec, 0)\n\t\tFROM spectra s\n\t\tJOIN markers m ON s.marker_id = m.id\n\t\tWHERE m.trackid = $1\n\t\t  AND s.channels IS NOT NULL\n\t\t  AND s.channels != ''\n\t\t  AND s.channels != '[]'\n\t\tORDER BY s.id`,\n\t\ttrackID,\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"query spectra: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar spectra []spectrumRow\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar sr spectrumRow\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&sr.id, &sr.markerID, &sr.channelsJSON, &sr.liveTimeSec); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif sr.liveTimeSec < cfg.SpectrumMinLiveTime {\n\t\t\tcontinue // too short — unreliable\n\t\t}\n\t\tspectra = append(spectra, sr)\n\t}\n\n\tsummary := &SpectrumAnomalySummary{\n\t\tTrackID:         trackID,\n\t\tComputedAt:      time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\tCosineThreshold: cfg.SpectrumCosineThresh,\n\t\tSpectrumCount:   len(spectra),\n\t}\n\n\tif len(spectra) < 2 {\n\t\t// Need at least 2 spectra to compare.\n\t\treturn summary, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse and normalise spectra to counts/second.\n\ttype normSpectrum struct {\n\t\tid          int64\n\t\tmarkerID    int64\n\t\tnormalised  []float64 // counts/sec per channel\n\t\ttotalCPS    float64\n\t}\n\n\tvar normalised []normSpectrum\n\tfor _, sr := range spectra {\n\t\tvar channels []int\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sr.channelsJSON), &channels); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif len(channels) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tns := normSpectrum{id: sr.id, markerID: sr.markerID}\n\t\tns.normalised = make([]float64, len(channels))\n\t\tfor i, c := range channels {\n\t\t\tcps := float64(c) / sr.liveTimeSec\n\t\t\tns.normalised[i] = cps\n\t\t\tns.totalCPS += cps\n\t\t}\n\t\tnormalised = append(normalised, ns)\n\t}\n\tsummary.SpectrumCount = len(normalised)\n\n\tif len(normalised) < 2 {\n\t\treturn summary, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Compute median total CPS across all spectra.\n\ttotalCPSValues := make([]float64, len(normalised))\n\tfor i, ns := range normalised {\n\t\ttotalCPSValues[i] = ns.totalCPS\n\t}\n\tsort.Float64s(totalCPSValues)\n\tn := len(totalCPSValues)\n\tif n%2 == 0 {\n\t\tsummary.MedianTotalCounts = (totalCPSValues[n/2-1] + totalCPSValues[n/2]) / 2\n\t} else {\n\t\tsummary.MedianTotalCounts = totalCPSValues[n/2]\n\t}\n\n\t// Compute the median spectrum (median CPS per channel).\n\t// All spectra must have the same channel count for this to be meaningful;\n\t// use the most common channel count and skip outliers.\n\tchannelCounts := make(map[int]int)\n\tfor _, ns := range normalised {\n\t\tchannelCounts[len(ns.normalised)]++\n\t}\n\tdominantLen := 0\n\tdominantCount := 0\n\tfor l, c := range channelCounts {\n\t\tif c > dominantCount {\n\t\t\tdominantLen, dominantCount = l, c\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter to spectra with the dominant channel count.\n\tvar filtered []normSpectrum\n\tfor _, ns := range normalised {\n\t\tif len(ns.normalised) == dominantLen {\n\t\t\tfiltered = append(filtered, ns)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(filtered) < 2 {\n\t\treturn summary, nil\n\t}\n\tsummary.SpectrumCount = len(filtered)\n\n\t// Build median spectrum channel-by-channel.\n\tmedianSpectrum := make([]float64, dominantLen)\n\tchannelVals := make([]float64, len(filtered))\n\tfor ch := 0; ch < dominantLen; ch++ {\n\t\tfor i, ns := range filtered {\n\t\t\tchannelVals[i] = ns.normalised[ch]\n\t\t}\n\t\tsort.Float64s(channelVals)\n\t\tm := len(channelVals)\n\t\tif m%2 == 0 {\n\t\t\tmedianSpectrum[ch] = (channelVals[m/2-1] + channelVals[m/2]) / 2\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tmedianSpectrum[ch] = channelVals[m/2]\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Cosine similarity of each spectrum against the median.\n\tmedianNorm := vectorNorm(medianSpectrum)\n\tif medianNorm == 0 {\n\t\treturn summary, nil // degenerate median — all zeros\n\t}\n\n\tvar anomalyIDs []int64\n\tvar anomalyDetails []spectrumAnomalyItem\n\n\tfor _, ns := range filtered {\n\t\tsim := cosineSimilarityVec(ns.normalised, medianSpectrum, medianNorm)\n\t\tif sim < cfg.SpectrumCosineThresh {\n\t\t\tanomalyIDs = append(anomalyIDs, ns.id)\n\t\t\tanomalyDetails = append(anomalyDetails, spectrumAnomalyItem{\n\t\t\t\tSpectrumID:        ns.id,\n\t\t\t\tMarkerID:          ns.markerID,\n\t\t\t\tCosineSimilarity:  sim,\n\t\t\t\tTotalCountsPerSec: ns.totalCPS,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Cap stored IDs per config.\n\tif len(anomalyIDs) > cfg.MaxAnomalyIDs {\n\t\tanomalyIDs = anomalyIDs[:cfg.MaxAnomalyIDs]\n\t\tanomalyDetails = anomalyDetails[:cfg.MaxAnomalyIDs]\n\t}\n\n\tsummary.AnomalyCount = len(anomalyIDs)\n\tsummary.AnomalySpectrumIDs = anomalyIDs\n\tsummary.AnomalyDetails = anomalyDetails\n\treturn summary, nil\n}\n\n// vectorNorm returns the L2 norm of a float64 slice.\nfunc vectorNorm(v []float64) float64 {\n\tvar sum float64\n\tfor _, x := range v {\n\t\tsum += x * x\n\t}\n\treturn math.Sqrt(sum)\n}\n\n// cosineSimilarityVec computes cosine similarity between a and b.\n// bNorm is pre-computed to avoid recalculating for every spectrum.\nfunc cosineSimilarityVec(a, b []float64, bNorm float64) float64 {\n\tif len(a) != len(b) || bNorm == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tvar dot float64\n\tfor i := range a {\n\t\tdot += a[i] * b[i]\n\t}\n\taNorm := vectorNorm(a)\n\tif aNorm == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn dot / (aNorm * bNorm)\n}\n\n// storeSpectrumAnomalySummary persists the summary using DELETE + INSERT.\nfunc storeSpectrumAnomalySummary(s *SpectrumAnomalySummary) error {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"duckdb not available\")\n\t}\n\n\tidsJSON, _ := json.Marshal(s.AnomalySpectrumIDs)\n\tdetailsJSON, _ := json.Marshal(s.AnomalyDetails)\n\n\tif _, err := duckDB.Exec(\n\t\t`DELETE FROM spectrum_anomaly_summaries WHERE track_id = ?`, s.TrackID,\n\t); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"delete old summary: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif _, err := duckDB.Exec(\n\t\t`INSERT INTO spectrum_anomaly_summaries\n\t\t (track_id, computed_at, spectrum_count, anomaly_count,\n\t\t  anomaly_spectrum_ids, median_total_counts, cosine_threshold, anomaly_details)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\ts.TrackID, s.ComputedAt, s.SpectrumCount, s.AnomalyCount,\n\t\tstring(idsJSON), s.MedianTotalCounts, s.CosineThreshold, string(detailsJSON),\n\t); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"insert summary: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// loadSpectrumAnomalySummary fetches the stored summary for a track.\n// Returns nil, nil if not yet computed.\nfunc loadSpectrumAnomalySummary(trackID string) (*SpectrumAnomalySummary, error) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\trow := duckDB.QueryRow(`\n\t\tSELECT track_id, computed_at, spectrum_count, anomaly_count,\n\t\t       anomaly_spectrum_ids, median_total_counts, cosine_threshold, anomaly_details\n\t\tFROM spectrum_anomaly_summaries\n\t\tWHERE track_id = ?\n\t\tLIMIT 1`,\n\t\ttrackID,\n\t)\n\tvar s SpectrumAnomalySummary\n\tvar idsJSON, detailsJSON string\n\tif err := row.Scan(\n\t\t&s.TrackID, &s.ComputedAt, &s.SpectrumCount, &s.AnomalyCount,\n\t\t&idsJSON, &s.MedianTotalCounts, &s.CosineThreshold, &detailsJSON,\n\t); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(idsJSON), &s.AnomalySpectrumIDs)\n\t_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(detailsJSON), &s.AnomalyDetails)\n\treturn &s, nil\n}\n"
}
```

  "new_string": "// adminSpectrumAnomalyDataHandler — GET /api/admin/anomaly/spectrum-data\n// Returns paginated rows from spectrum_anomaly_summaries in DuckLake.\nfunc adminSpectrumAnomalyDataHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Analytics not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlimit, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"limit\"))\n\tif limit <= 0 || limit > 500 {\n\t\tlimit = 50\n\t}\n\toffset, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"offset\"))\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\n\tvalidCols := []string{\"track_id\", \"computed_at\", \"spectrum_count\", \"anomaly_count\", \"median_total_counts\"}\n\tsortCol := r.URL.Query().Get(\"sort\")\n\tif !isValidColumn(sortCol, validCols) {\n\t\tsortCol = \"computed_at\"\n\t}\n\torder := strings.ToUpper(r.URL.Query().Get(\"order\"))\n\tif order != \"ASC\" {\n\t\torder = \"DESC\"\n\t}\n\n\tsearch := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\"))\n\twhereSQL := \"\"\n\tvar args []interface{}\n\tif search != \"\" {\n\t\twhereSQL = \"WHERE track_id LIKE ?\"\n\t\targs = append(args, \"%\"+search+\"%\")\n\t}\n\n\tvar total int\n\tcountQuery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM spectrum_anomaly_summaries %s\", whereSQL)\n\tif err := duckDB.QueryRow(countQuery, args...).Scan(&total); err != nil {\n\t\ttotal = 0\n\t}\n\n\tdataQuery := fmt.Sprintf(`\n\t\tSELECT track_id, computed_at, spectrum_count, anomaly_count,\n\t\t       median_total_counts, cosine_threshold\n\t\tFROM spectrum_anomaly_summaries %s\n\t\tORDER BY %s %s\n\t\tLIMIT %d OFFSET %d`,\n\t\twhereSQL, sortCol, order, limit, offset)\n\n\trows, err := duckDB.Query(dataQuery, args...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[spectrum-anomaly-admin] query: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Query failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar results []map[string]interface{}\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar (\n\t\t\ttrackID                    string\n\t\t\tcomputedAt                 interface{}\n\t\t\tspectrumCount, anomalyCount int\n\t\t\tmedianTotal, cosineThresh  float64\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&trackID, &computedAt, &spectrumCount, &anomalyCount,\n\t\t\t&medianTotal, &cosineThresh); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults = append(results, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"track_id\":           trackID,\n\t\t\t\"computed_at\":        computedAt,\n\t\t\t\"spectrum_count\":     spectrumCount,\n\t\t\t\"anomaly_count\":      anomalyCount,\n\t\t\t\"median_total_counts\": medianTotal,\n\t\t\t\"cosine_threshold\":   cosineThresh,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\tif results == nil {\n\t\tresults = []map[string]interface{}{}\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{ //nolint:errcheck\n\t\t\"data\":  results,\n\t\t\"total\": total,\n\t})\n}\n\n// adminAnomalyRerunHandler — POST /api/admin/anomaly/rerun/{trackID}"
}

  "new_string": "// adminAnomalyRerunHandler — POST /api/admin/anomaly/rerun/{trackID}\n// Triggers re-computation of dose-rate anomaly analysis for a single track.\nfunc adminAnomalyRerunHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tparts := strings.Split(strings.Trim(r.URL.Path, \"/\"), \"/\")\n\ttrackID := parts[len(parts)-1]\n\tif trackID == \"\" || trackID == \"rerun\" {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"track_id required\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tsummary, err := computeAnomalies(trackID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly] rerun %s: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := storeAnomalySummary(summary); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly] rerun store %s: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[anomaly] rerun %s: %d readings, %d anomalies\", trackID, summary.ReadingCount, summary.AnomalyCount)\n\t}()\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{\"status\": \"queued\", \"track_id\": trackID}) //nolint:errcheck\n}\n\n// adminSpectrumAnomalyRerunHandler — POST /api/admin/anomaly/spectrum-rerun/{trackID}\n// Triggers re-computation of spectrum anomaly analysis for a single track.\nfunc adminSpectrumAnomalyRerunHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tparts := strings.Split(strings.Trim(r.URL.Path, \"/\"), \"/\")\n\ttrackID := parts[len(parts)-1]\n\tif trackID == \"\" || trackID == \"spectrum-rerun\" {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"track_id required\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tsummary, err := computeSpectrumAnomalies(trackID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[spectrum-anomaly] rerun %s: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := storeSpectrumAnomalySummary(summary); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[spectrum-anomaly] rerun store %s: %v\", trackID, err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[spectrum-anomaly] rerun %s: %d spectra, %d anomalies\", trackID, summary.SpectrumCount, summary.AnomalyCount)\n\t}()\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{\"status\": \"queued\", \"track_id\": trackID}) //nolint:errcheck\n}"

{

  "new_string": "// ── Spectrum anomaly table ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\nlet specSort = 'computed_at', specOrder = 'desc', specSearch = '', specOffset = 0, specLimit = 50, specTotal = 0, specData = [];\n\nfunction doSpecSearch() { specSearch = document.getElementById('specSearchInput').value.trim(); specOffset = 0; fetchSpecData(); }\nfunction clearSpecSearch() { document.getElementById('specSearchInput').value=''; specSearch=''; specOffset=0; fetchSpecData(); }\n\nfunction specSortBy(col) {\n  if (specSort === col) { specOrder = specOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc'; }\n  else { specSort = col; specOrder = 'desc'; }\n  specOffset = 0; fetchSpecData(); updateSpecSortIndicators();\n}\n\nfunction updateSpecSortIndicators() {\n  document.querySelectorAll('#specTable th.sortable').forEach(th => {\n    th.classList.remove('asc','desc');\n    const col = th.getAttribute('onclick')?.match(/specSortBy\\('(\\w+)'\\)/)?.[1];\n    if (col === specSort) th.classList.add(specOrder);\n  });\n}\n\nasync function fetchSpecData() {\n  const loading = document.getElementById('specLoading');\n  const table   = document.getElementById('specTable');\n  const noData  = document.getElementById('specNoData');\n  loading.style.display='block'; table.style.display='none'; noData.style.display='none';\n\n  let url = `/api/admin/anomaly/spectrum-data?limit=${specLimit}&offset=${specOffset}&sort=${specSort}&order=${specOrder}`;\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n  if (specSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(specSearch);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url);\n    if (!resp.ok) { loading.textContent = 'HTTP ' + resp.status; return; }\n    const json = await resp.json();\n    specTotal = json.total; specData = json.data || [];\n    loading.style.display = 'none';\n    if (specData.length === 0) { noData.style.display='block'; document.getElementById('specSummary').textContent='No results'; document.getElementById('specPagination').innerHTML=''; return; }\n\n    const tbody = document.getElementById('specTableBody');\n    tbody.innerHTML = specData.map((row, idx) => `\n      <tr>\n        <td><code>${row.track_id}</code></td>\n        <td>${formatDate(row.computed_at)}</td>\n        <td>${row.spectrum_count.toLocaleString()}</td>\n        <td>${anomalyBadge(row.anomaly_count)}</td>\n        <td>${fmt(row.median_total_counts, 1)}</td>\n        <td>${fmt(row.cosine_threshold, 2)}</td>\n        <td>\n          <a class=\"btn-view\" href=\"/trackid/${row.track_id}\" target=\"_blank\">View</a>\n          <button class=\"btn-rerun\" id=\"spec-rerun-${idx}\" onclick=\"specRerun('${row.track_id}',${idx})\">Re-run</button>\n        </td>\n      </tr>`).join('');\n\n    table.style.display='table'; updateSpecSortIndicators();\n    const page = Math.floor(specOffset/specLimit)+1, totalPages = Math.ceil(specTotal/specLimit);\n    document.getElementById('specSummary').textContent = `${specTotal} track${specTotal!==1?'s':''} analysed | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;\n    buildSpecPagination(totalPages, page);\n  } catch(err) { loading.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message; }\n}\n\nasync function specRerun(trackID, idx) {\n  const btn = document.getElementById('spec-rerun-' + idx);\n  if (btn) { btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='...'; }\n  let url = `/api/admin/anomaly/spectrum-rerun/${trackID}` + AUTH_PARAM;\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST' });\n    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    setTimeout(() => fetchSpecData(), 4000);\n  } catch(e) {\n    alert('Re-run failed: ' + e.message);\n    if (btn) { btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='Re-run'; }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction buildSpecPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {\n  const container = document.getElementById('specPagination');\n  let html = `<button ${currentPage<=1?'disabled':''} onclick=\"goSpecPage(1)\">First</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage<=1?'disabled':''} onclick=\"goSpecPage(${currentPage-1})\">Prev</button>`;\n  let start=Math.max(1,currentPage-3), end=Math.min(totalPages,start+6); start=Math.max(1,end-6);\n  for (let i=start;i<=end;i++) html+=`<button class=\"${i===currentPage?'active':''}\" onclick=\"goSpecPage(${i})\">${i}</button>`;\n  html+=`<button ${currentPage>=totalPages?'disabled':''} onclick=\"goSpecPage(${currentPage+1})\">Next</button>`;\n  html+=`<button ${currentPage>=totalPages?'disabled':''} onclick=\"goSpecPage(${totalPages})\">Last</button>`;\n  html+=` <select onchange=\"changeSpecLimit(this.value)\">${[25,50,100,200].map(n=>`<option value=\"${n}\" ${n===specLimit?'selected':''}>${n} per page</option>`).join('')}</select>`;\n  container.innerHTML = html;\n}\nfunction goSpecPage(p) { specOffset=(p-1)*specLimit; fetchSpecData(); }\nfunction changeSpecLimit(v) { specLimit=parseInt(v); specOffset=0; fetchSpecData(); }\n\nbuildAdminTabs();\nloadConfig();\nfetchData();\nfetchSpecData();"
}

> AGENT

Clean build. Here's what's been added:

**Spectrum anomaly detection:**
- Each spectrum normalised to counts/second (removes live-time bias)
- Median spectrum computed per channel across all spectra in the track
- Each spectrum's cosine similarity vs the median computed — catches shape changes (new isotopes, interference) independent of overall intensity
- Spectra below the threshold flagged with their similarity score and total CPS

**Admin page (`/admin/anomaly`)** now has:
- **Spectrum Detection Settings** card — cosine threshold (default 0.85) and min live time (default 1s)
- **Spectrum Track Anomalies** table below the dose-rate table — same sortable/paginated style with Re-run per row

**To test:** restart the server, upload a `.rctrk` or `.n42` spectrum file, watch logs for `[spectrum-anomaly] <trackID>: N spectra, M anomalies`, then check the Anomalies admin page.

Now I have enough to piece together a solid analysis. Here's what I found, Rob:

## How the Radiaverse / RadiaMaps H3 Grid Layer Works

Based on what I could gather (the site blocks automated fetching, so this is reconstructed from the FAQ, ToS, UI elements, and the RadiaMaps interface), here's the architecture:

### 1. H3 Resolution & Zoom Mapping

The RadiaMaps UI exposes a `HEX_RESOLUTION` control with an "Auto (Adjusts with Zoom)" mode and manual overrides from resolution 3 (world scale) through 15, with named levels: 3=WORLD, 4=CONTINENTAL, 5=COUNTRY, 6=REGION, 7=CITY, 8=DISTRICT, 9=BLOCK, 10=BUILDING, then 11–15 unnamed. So the hexagon grid dynamically adjusts resolution as you zoom in/out — this is the standard multi-resolution H3 approach where the map viewport zoom level is mapped to an appropriate H3 resolution.

### 2. Data Pipeline (Server-Side)

The backend does the heavy lifting:

- **Ingestion**: Users upload track files from RadiaCode devices. The system calculates derived data including speed and H3 indices from the raw GPS + dose rate readings.
- **Speed filtering**: Readings taken at over 100 mph are filtered out of the hexagon averaging algorithm — this removes noisy highway/aircraft data that would skew the averages.
- **H3 indexing**: Each valid reading's lat/lng is converted to an H3 cell index at the relevant resolution(s) using `latLngToCell()`. The backend likely pre-computes indices at multiple resolutions, or computes parent cells on the fly from a fine-resolution index.
- **Aggregation**: All valid uploaded data is always used anonymously to calculate the aggregated average dose information displayed in the map's hexagons. So each hexagon shows the mean dose rate (in µSv/h) of all readings that fall within that cell.

### 3. Frontend Rendering

The map is most likely built with **Mapbox GL JS** (the UI shows Mapbox-style map style options like Satellite, Outdoors, Streets, Navigation Day/Night) combined with either:

- **deck.gl's `H3HexagonLayer`** — the most common approach for rendering H3 cells on Mapbox. It takes an array of `{h3index, value}` objects and renders filled/stroked hexagons with color mapping. This is very efficient for large datasets since it can use instanced rendering.
- Or a custom GeoJSON layer where hexagon boundaries are computed from H3 indices using `cellToBoundary()` from h3-js.

Given the cyberpunk styling and the performance needed for potentially hundreds of thousands of hexagons globally, deck.gl's H3HexagonLayer is the most probable choice.

### 4. Color Mapping

The hexagons are color-coded by aggregated dose rate, likely using a gradient from green/blue (low background, ~0.05 µSv/h) through yellow/orange to red (elevated levels). The UI also supports toggling hexagons on/off and switching dose rate units between nSv/h, µSv/h, and mSv/h.

### 5. Two-Layer System

The UI has separate toggles for "Show Readings" and "Show Hexagons", indicating two distinct layers: raw individual data points (scatter/dot layer) and the aggregated H3 hexagon overlay. The readings layer also has a `READINGS_LIMIT` control (All, None, 20k–200k) for performance management.

### Key Technical Differences from Safecast's Approach

This is interesting to compare with Safecast's tilemap approach. RadiaMaps/Radiaverse uses H3 for spatial binning rather than Safecast's traditional raster tile approach. The advantages are the multi-resolution hierarchy (zoom in → finer hexagons automatically), equidistant neighbors for cleaner spatial analysis, and the ability to do server-side pre-aggregation by H3 index which maps efficiently to database queries (e.g., `GROUP BY h3_index`).

Want me to dig deeper into any specific aspect, or perhaps look at how something similar could be implemented for Safecast's data?
